##### Commands & Extensions

* [US Watchlist Alerts (⭐2)](https://github.com/eli-alvarado/us-watchlist-alerts) ⭐ 2 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-19 - Agent Skills (SKILL.md) for a US stock watchlist: NYSE-aware, de-duplicated price alerts (levels, % moves, gaps, 52-week highs/lows, MA crosses, RSI, earnings) and morning/close briefs. Stdlib Python, no API key, never places trades. MIT. Install with `npx skills add eli-alvarado/us-watchlist-alerts -y -a gemini-cli`.

##### Development Tools & Utilities

* [laya-guard (⭐30)](https://github.com/scs0209/laya-guard) ⭐ 30 | 🐛 18 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-19 - Local guardrail with a `BeforeTool` hook for Gemini CLI (`laya-guard install gemini-cli`) that judges each shell command before it runs, using regex rules, protected paths, and a small on-device model. Blocked commands are denied; ones marked for review pass with a note. Also hooks into Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, and other agents. MIT.
* [ChatVault (⭐26)](https://github.com/ssanaullahrais/Chat-Vault) ⭐ 28 | 🐛 5 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-10-18 - Windows app that reads, searches and backs up your Gemini CLI chats across accounts, and continues any chat in Gemini CLI through a handoff file that carries the whole conversation.

#### [6. Collective Ai Tools](/content/Hyraze/collective-ai-tools/README.md)

##### Audio & Speech

* [Remove Audio](https://remove-audio.com/tools/extract-voice-from-video) - Extracts clean dialogue or vocals from a video with an AI model that runs in the browser, so the file is not uploaded. `#freemium`

##### Data & BI

* [DC Hub](https://dchub.cloud/) - DC Hub is the live data layer for data-center site selection. Its Data Center Power Index (DCPI) is a daily score of where data centers can get grid power, across 300+ markets. Add <https://dchub.cloud/mcp> as an MCP server in Claude, ChatGPT or Cursor. `#freemium` `#mcp`

##### Dating

* [EroLove](https://erolove.ai/) - AI roleplay chat for adults, with memory across conversations, ready-made scenes, and group chats with up to four characters. `#freemium`

##### Developer Tools

* [The Enso Method](https://github.com/EnsoDynamics/enso-method) ⭐ 5 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-19 - Agent skills for Claude Code and Codex that record business assumptions with evidence and confidence, and preserve requirements as the source of truth. `#free` `#opensource`
* [Aident Loadout](https://aident.ai/) - Connects Claude Code, Codex, Cursor and other MCP clients to your apps and 400+ expert-built Skills through one remote MCP server, CLI or Skill, with credentials kept in a vault and every action logged. `#freemium` `#mcp`
* [CurlShot](https://curlshot.com/) - Screenshot API that captures any URL, HTML, or Markdown as PNG, JPEG, WebP, PDF, or scrolling video, with a hosted MCP server so AI agents can take screenshots. Free plan with 100 screenshots/month. `#freemium` `#api` `#mcp`
* [ShellWire](https://shellwire.com/) - Software discovery directory with explainable utility rankings, source-backed tool comparisons, and a remote Streamable HTTP MCP server for AI assistants. `#free`

##### Human Resources

* [CareJobs.Now](https://carejobs.now) - Free AI job search for US healthcare workers: describe the job you want in plain words and it matches you to 170,000+ hospital and clinic jobs with posted pay. `#free`

##### Image Editing

* [BatchSet](https://batchset.com/tools/ai-background-removal) - Remove image backgrounds with AI right in your browser, then convert, compress or resize whole folders at once. Free without signup. `#freemium`

##### Image Generator

* [Muse Studio](https://muse-me-avatar-beta.zhangwei798879.chatgpt.site/create) - Generates static ad concepts from text with editable headline, offer and CTA, and PNG export. Limited free beta; ChatGPT sign-in; no card. `#free` `#design`

##### Models

* [ArtificialWatch](https://artificialwatch.com/) - AI model release tracker that checks provider APIs every 60 seconds and alerts you by email, push, SMS or phone call when a new model goes live. `#freemium`

##### SEO

* [AltoRank](https://altorank.co/) - Open-source SEO content software that researches what your buyers search for and drafts sourced articles; self-host it free or use the hosted version. `#freemium` `#seo` `#opensource`

#### [17. Awesome Gemini Cli](/content/Piebald-AI/awesome-gemini-cli/README.md)

##### Commands & Extensions

* [Gemini Safety Net (⭐3)](https://github.com/kenryu42/gemini-safety-net) ⭐ 3 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-09-29 - Gemini CLI extension that runs [cc-safety-net (⭐1.7k)](https://github.com/kenryu42/cc-safety-net) ⭐ 1,673 | 🐛 1 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-10-19 as a `BeforeTool` hook. It blocks destructive Git and filesystem commands, and reads of secrets such as SSH keys and `.env` files, before Gemini runs the tool call. It parses the command, so wrapped forms such as `bash -c` are also checked. It is not a sandbox. MIT. Install with `gemini extensions install https://github.com/kenryu42/gemini-safety-net`.
* [Kuudo Skills (⭐2)](https://github.com/KuudoAI/skills) ⭐ 2 | 🐛 3 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-19 - Gemini CLI extension with 22 Agent Skills for Amazon sellers and advertisers: Sponsored Products and Brands audits, search-term mining, budget pacing, DSP and Amazon Marketing Cloud, listings, product images, repricing, FBA inbound, and profitability, built for Kuudo's hosted Amazon Ads and SP-API MCP servers. Price, budget, listing, and shipment writes are previewed and wait for your approval. Install with `gemini extensions install https://github.com/KuudoAI/skills`. PolyForm Shield 1.0.0 (source-available).

##### Development Tools & Utilities

* [SafetyCard (⭐3)](https://github.com/safetycard/safetycard) ⭐ 3 | 🐛 1 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-18 - Safety-check MCP servers and AI agents against the OWASP agentic Top 10, and get the safest setup to run them. Install with `npx skills add safetycard/safetycard --agent gemini-cli`.

* [Thinking Methods (⭐15)](https://github.com/ysskrishna/ai-agent-skills) ⭐ 17 | 🐛 1 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-16 - Curated Agent Skills (SKILL.md) for structured thinking on real decisions: five whys, pre-mortem, trade-offs, prioritization, six thinking hats, and twelve more methods plus a selector skill. Install as a Gemini extension (`gemini extensions install https://github.com/ysskrishna/ai-agent-skills`) or skills only (`gemini skills install https://github.com/ysskrishna/ai-agent-skills.git --path skills`). MIT.

##### Development Tools & Utilities

* [agentvcr (⭐2)](https://github.com/tojacob03/agentvcr) ⭐ 2 | 🐛 1 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-10-17 - Turns a Gemini CLI chat from `~/.gemini/tmp/*/chats` (the JSONL format with rewinds and patches, or the older JSON files) into a self-contained HTML replay with a scrubbable timeline, diffs and terminal output, with API keys and other secrets masked locally first. Share it as a link through a secret gist on your own GitHub, embed it, export a Markdown transcript for a pull request, or have its GitHub Action comment the replay on agent PRs. Also reads Claude Code, Codex, Cursor and OpenCode. MIT, no dependencies. Run: `npx agentvcr-cli`.
